    let effectiveStrengthDiscountPercent: number | null = null;
    let selectedWeaknessPercent: number | null = null;
    if (o.cost !== null) {
      const c = o.cost;
      const d = data(c.stepKey);
      const net = data(c.netStepKey);
      const outcomes = net === null ? [] : (own(net, \"outcomes\") as Record<string, unknown>[]);
      const netTargets = net === null ? [] : (own(net, \"finalTargets\") as Record<string, unknown>[]);
      const finalTargets = d === null ? [] : (own(d, \"finalTargets\") as Record<string, unknown>[]);
      const stage = (modifierKey: string | null): AffinityCostStage | null => {
        if (modifierKey === null) return null;
        const x = outcomes.find((y) => own(y, \"modifierKey\") === modifierKey);
        if (x === undefined) return null;
        return { modifierKey, operation: own(x, \"operation\") as string, percent: own(x, \"operand\") as number, applied: own(x, \"status\") === \"APPLIED\", before: own(x, \"before\") as number, after: own(x, \"after\") as number };
      };
      const strength = stage(c.strengthModifierKey);
      const weakness = stage(c.weaknessModifierKey);
      const selection = o.weaknessSelection === null ? null : data(o.weaknessSelection);
      effectiveStrengthDiscountPercent = strength !== null && strength.applied ? strength.percent : 0;
      selectedWeaknessPercent = selection === null ? 0 : (own((own(selection, \"finalTargets\") as Record<string, unknown>[])[0] as Record<string, unknown>, \"value\") as number);
      const target = finalTargets.find((t) => own(t, \"targetKey\") === c.targetKey);
      const netTarget = netTargets[0];
      if (target !== undefined && netTarget !== undefined) cost = { costTarget: c.costTarget, baseCost: c.baseCost, strength, weakness, netCostPercent: own(netTarget, \"value\") as number, affinityAdjustedCost: own(target, \"value\") as number };
    }
    alignment = {
      governingAffinity: o.governing,
      alignment: anyStrength && anyWeakness ? \"STRENGTH_AND_WEAKNESS\" : anyStrength ? \"STRENGTH\" : anyWeakness ? \"WEAKNESS\" : \"NEUTRAL\",
      strengths,
      weaknesses,
      effectiveStrengthDiscountPercent,
      selectedWeaknessPercent,
      issues: [],
    };
  }

  let authority: AffinityAuthorityReading | null = null;
  if (o.authority !== null) {
    const a = o.authority;
    const satisfied = (stepKey: string) => own(data(stepKey) ?? {}, \"satisfied\") === true;
    const effectCapacity = a.effectCapacity === null ? null : { capacity: a.effectCapacity.capacity, count: a.effectCapacity.count, satisfied: satisfied(a.effectCapacity.stepKey) };
    const traitCapacity = a.traitCapacity === null ? null : { capacity: a.traitCapacity.capacity, count: a.traitCapacity.count, satisfied: satisfied(a.traitCapacity.stepKey) };
    const effectAccess = a.effectAccess.map((x) => ({ componentKey: x.componentKey, satisfied: satisfied(x.stepKey), granted: x.granted }));
    const rankSatisfied = satisfied(a.rankStep);
    authority = {
      authority: a.authority,
      minimumRank: a.minimumRank,
      rankSatisfied,
      effectCapacity,
      traitCapacity,
      effectAccess,
      valid: rankSatisfied && effectCapacity !== null && effectCapacity.satisfied && traitCapacity !== null && traitCapacity.satisfied && effectAccess.every((x) => x.satisfied),
    };
  }
  const access = o.access.map((x) => ({ requirementKey: x.requirementKey, affinity: x.affinity, minimumRank: x.minimumRank, satisfied: own(data(x.stepKey) ?? {}, \"satisfied\") === true }));
  return finish(p, () => deepFreeze({ alignment, cost, authority, access }));
}

import { createRulesetContext, findResolvedRule, type RulesetContext } from \"@prowess/rules\";
import { Problems, checkPlainData, finish, own, readRecord, type ProwessOutcome } from \"../internal/problems.js\";
import { BUNDLE_ERROR_CODES as B, DEFINITION_ERROR_CODES as C } from \"../definition/codes.js\";
import { definitionRefOf, isSameExactDefinition, readEnvelope, type ProwessDefinitionEnvelope, type ProwessDefinitionRef } from \"../definition/envelope.js\";
import { PROWESS_DEFINITION_KINDS } from \"../vocabulary/families.js\";

/** The exact Ruleset identity a bundle was materialized from (PAS-10 M5-WO1 §84), copied verbatim from the context. */
export interface ProwessRulesetProvenance {
  readonly rulesetId: string;
  readonly manifestId: string;
  readonly manifestHash: string | null;
  readonly rulesetVersionLabel: string | null;
  readonly release: { readonly releaseId: string; readonly releaseNumber: number; readonly versionLabel: string } | null;
}

/**
 * Every exact Prowess Definition of ONE exact RulesetContext (§84–87, M5-I007–I009). Definitions are ordered by
 * Entity id (the RulesetContext order) so equal inputs give deep-equal bundles whatever order they were supplied in.
 * A bundle never holds a version its RulesetContext does not pin, and never mixes two Rulesets.
 */
export interface ProwessDefinitionBundle {
  readonly rulesetProvenance: ProwessRulesetProvenance;
  readonly definitions: readonly ProwessDefinitionEnvelope[];
}

export function rulesetProvenanceOf(ruleset: RulesetContext): ProwessRulesetProvenance {
  return {
    rulesetId: ruleset.rulesetId,
    manifestId: ruleset.manifestId,
    manifestHash: ruleset.manifestHash,
    rulesetVersionLabel: ruleset.rulesetVersionLabel,
    release: ruleset.release === null ? null : { ...ruleset.release },
  };
}

/**
 * Builds a bundle from an exact RulesetContext and Definition envelopes. Fails closed on: unsafe data, malformed or
 * unsupported envelopes, a version the context does not pin (injection), a canonical-key or EntityType disagreement
 * with the pinned rule, provenance naming another Ruleset / Manifest / Release (mixing), and duplicates.
 */
export function createProwessDefinitionBundle(input: unknown): ProwessOutcome<ProwessDefinitionBundle> {
  const p = new Problems();
  if (!checkPlainData(input, [], p, C.NOT_PLAIN_DATA)) return finish(p, () => null as never);
  const r = readRecord(input, [], p, B.INVALID_BUNDLE, [\"ruleset\", \"definitions\"], [\"ruleset\", \"definitions\"]);
  if (r === null) return finish(p, () => null as never);

  const context = createRulesetContext(own(r, \"ruleset\"));
  if (!context.ok) {
    p.add(B.INVALID_RULESET, [\"ruleset\"], \"a bundle requires an exact, valid M4 RulesetContext\");
    return finish(p, () => null as never);
  }
  const ruleset = context.value;
  const raw = own(r, \"definitions\");
  if (!Array.isArray(raw)) {
    p.add(B.INVALID_BUNDLE, [\"definitions\"], \"definitions is an array of Prowess Definition envelopes\");
    return finish(p, () => null as never);
  }

  const accepted: ProwessDefinitionEnvelope[] = [];
  raw.forEach((item: unknown, i: number) => {
    const at = [\"definitions\", i];
    const envelope = readEnvelope(item, at, p);
    if (envelope === null) return;
    const pinned = findResolvedRule(ruleset, envelope.entityId);
    if (pinned === null || pinned.entityVersionId !== envelope.entityVersionId) {
      p.add(B.NOT_PINNED, [...at, \"entityVersionId\"], \"this exact EntityVersion is not pinned by the bundle's RulesetContext; Definitions cannot be injected\");
      return;
    }
    if (pinned.canonicalKey !== envelope.canonicalKey) {
      p.add(B.CANONICAL_CONFLICT, [...at, \"canonicalKey\"], `the pinned Entity's canonical key is ${pinned.canonicalKey}`);
      return;
    }
    if (!PROWESS_DEFINITION_KINDS[envelope.definitionKind].entityTypes.includes(pinned.entityType)) {
      p.add(C.ENTITY_TYPE_MISMATCH, [...at, \"definitionKind\"], `${envelope.definitionKind} cannot be stored as EntityType ${pinned.entityType}`);
      return;
    }
    const pv = envelope.provenance;
    const releaseId = ruleset.release === null ? null : ruleset.release.releaseId;
    if (pv.rulesetId !== ruleset.rulesetId || pv.manifestId !== ruleset.manifestId || pv.releaseId !== releaseId) {
      p.add(B.RULESET_MISMATCH, [...at, \"provenance\"], \"this Definition was materialized under a different exact Ruleset / Manifest / Release; Rulesets are never mixed\");
      return;
    }
    if (pv.sourceRulesetId !== pinned.sourceRulesetId || pv.sourceManifestId !== pinned.sourceManifestId) {
      p.add(B.RULESET_MISMATCH, [...at, \"provenance\"], \"pin provenance must equal the exact context's (sourceRulesetId / sourceManifestId)\");
      return;
    }
    const ref = definitionRefOf(envelope);
    if (accepted.some((a) => isSameExactDefinition(definitionRefOf(a), ref))) {
      p.add(B.DUPLICATE_DEFINITION, at, \"the same exact Definition version is supplied twice\");
      return;
    }
    accepted.push(envelope);
  });

  return finish(p, () => ({
    rulesetProvenance: rulesetProvenanceOf(ruleset),
    definitions: accepted.sort((a, b) => (a.entityId < b.entityId ? -1 : a.entityId > b.entityId ? 1 : 0)),
  }));
}

/**
 * The bundle Definition `ref` names EXACTLY, or null. No fallback: absence never means \"use another version\", and no
 * display-name, alias, canonical-key-only or semantic-shape lookup exists (§79, §91).
 */
export function findBundleDefinition(bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle, ref: ProwessDefinitionRef): ProwessDefinitionEnvelope | null {
  return bundle.definitions.find((d) => isSameExactDefinition(definitionRefOf(d), ref)) ?? null;
}

/** Whether a bundle was built from exactly this RulesetContext's identity. */
export function bundleMatchesRuleset(bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle, ruleset: RulesetContext): boolean {
  const a = bundle.rulesetProvenance;
  const b = rulesetProvenanceOf(ruleset);
  const sameRelease =
    a.release === null || b.release === null
      ? a.release === b.release
      : a.release.releaseId === b.release.releaseId && a.release.releaseNumber === b.release.releaseNumber && a.release.versionLabel === b.release.versionLabel;
  return a.rulesetId === b.rulesetId && a.manifestId === b.manifestId && a.manifestHash === b.manifestHash && a.rulesetVersionLabel === b.rulesetVersionLabel && sameRelease;
}

import type { JsonObject } from \"@prowess/model\";
import { EVALUATION_CONTEXT_FAMILIES, EVALUATION_OPERATIONS, type EvaluationBinding, type EvaluationStep } from \"@prowess/rules\";
import { clonePlainData, isPlainObject, type PathSegment } from \"../internal/plain-data.js\";
import { Problems, checkPlainData, finish, isExactLabel, own, readEnum, readRecord, readSafeInteger, type ProwessOutcome } from \"../internal/problems.js\";
import { COMPILATION_ERROR_CODES as X, DEFINITION_ERROR_CODES as C } from \"../definition/codes.js\";
import { isSameExactDefinition, readDefinitionRef, type ProwessDefinitionRef } from \"../definition/envelope.js\";
import { findBundleDefinition, type ProwessDefinitionBundle } from \"../bundle/bundle.js\";
import { compositionComponents, type AbilityComposition } from \"../construction/composition.js\";
import { PROWESS_COMPILATION_CONTRACT_VERSION } from \"../contract-version.js\";
import type { CompilationFragment, ProwessCompiledPlan, ProwessSourceMapEntry } from \"./model.js\";

/**
 * Deterministic step identity (PAS-10 M5-WO1 §102, M5-I013): derived only from the AbilityKind, the Composition key and
 * the fragment's local key — never a random suffix, a clock value or an object identity.
 */
export function compiledStepKey(subjectKind: string, subjectKey: string, fragmentKey: string): string {
  return [\"prowess\", subjectKind, subjectKey, fragmentKey].join(\":\");
}

/**
 * The domain origin a fragment may cite: each component key of the subject and the exact Definition(s) it uses. For an
 * Ability this is the Composition's component uses; for a core-resolution subject (M5-WO2) it is the role-indexed
 * core Definitions the compiler resolved.
 */
export type SubjectComponents = ReadonlyMap<string, readonly ProwessDefinitionRef[]>;

export interface AssembledSteps {
  readonly steps: EvaluationStep[];
  readonly entries: ProwessSourceMapEntry[];
}

const FRAGMENT_FIELDS = [\"fragmentKey\", \"order\", \"operation\", \"input\", \"bindings\", \"role\", \"componentKeys\", \"definitions\"];

/**
 * Assembles component-compiler fragments into an M4 EvaluationPlan plus its source map. Pure assembly only (§106):
 * it copies authored input templates, rewrites fragment references to deterministic step keys and orders steps by
 * their explicit `order` (ties broken by stepKey, exactly as M4 executes them). It performs no calculation (§107).
 *
 * Every fragment must trace to at least one component of THIS Composition and only cite exact Definitions those
 * components use (M5-I014). Fragment references must point to an earlier-ordered fragment.
 */
export function assembleCompiledPlan(input: {
  readonly composition: AbilityComposition;
  readonly bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle;
  readonly fragments: readonly unknown[];
}): ProwessOutcome<ProwessCompiledPlan> {
  const { composition, bundle } = input;
  const components: SubjectComponents = new Map(compositionComponents(composition).map((c) => [c.use.componentKey, [c.use.definition]]));
  const p = new Problems();
  const assembled = assembleFragments({ subjectKind: composition.abilityKind, subjectKey: composition.compositionKey, components, bundle, fragments: input.fragments }, p);
  return finish(p, () => ({
    contractVersion: PROWESS_COMPILATION_CONTRACT_VERSION,
    subject: { kind: composition.abilityKind, compositionKey: composition.compositionKey },
    rulesetProvenance: bundle.rulesetProvenance,
    evaluationPlan: { steps: assembled.steps, primaryStepKey: null, validationStepKey: null, explanationStepKey: null },
    sourceMap: { entries: assembled.entries },
  }));
}

/**
 * Subject-neutral assembly shared by every Prowess compiler (Abilities in WO1, core resolution in WO2): validates the
 * fragments, maps every one to the subject's components and exact Definitions, rewrites fragment references to
 * deterministic step keys and orders steps exactly as M4 executes them. Problems are recorded in `p`.
 */
export function assembleFragments(
  input: { readonly subjectKind: string; readonly subjectKey: string; readonly components: SubjectComponents; readonly bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle; readonly fragments: readonly unknown[] },
  p: Problems,
): AssembledSteps {
  const { bundle } = input;
  if (!checkPlainData(input.fragments, [\"fragments\"], p, C.NOT_PLAIN_DATA)) return { steps: [], entries: [] };

  const uses = input.components;
  const fragments: CompilationFragment[] = [];
  const keys = new Map<string, number>();

  input.fragments.forEach((raw: unknown, i: number) => {
    const at: PathSegment[] = [\"fragments\", i];
    const r = readRecord(raw, at, p, X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, FRAGMENT_FIELDS, FRAGMENT_FIELDS);
    if (r === null) return;
    const before = p.count;
    const fragmentKey = own(r, \"fragmentKey\");
    if (!isExactLabel(fragmentKey)) p.add(X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, [...at, \"fragmentKey\"], \"fragmentKey is an exact label\");
    else if (keys.has(fragmentKey)) p.add(X.DUPLICATE_STEP, [...at, \"fragmentKey\"], `fragmentKey \"${fragmentKey}\" is emitted twice`);
    const order = readSafeInteger(own(r, \"order\"), [...at, \"order\"], p, X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, \"order\");
    const operation = readEnum(own(r, \"operation\"), EVALUATION_OPERATIONS, [...at, \"operation\"], p, X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, \"M4 operation\");
    const role = own(r, \"role\");
    if (!isExactLabel(role)) p.add(X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, [...at, \"role\"], \"role is an exact label\");
    const template = own(r, \"input\");
    if (!isPlainObject(template)) p.add(X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, [...at, \"input\"], \"input is a plain-data M4 operation template\");

    const componentKeys = own(r, \"componentKeys\");
    if (!Array.isArray(componentKeys) || componentKeys.length === 0 || !componentKeys.every((k: unknown) => typeof k === \"string\" && uses.has(k))) {
      p.add(X.UNMAPPED_SOURCE, [...at, \"componentKeys\"], \"every compiled step names at least one component of this Composition\");
    }
    const rawDefinitions = own(r, \"definitions\");
    const definitions: ProwessDefinitionRef[] = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(rawDefinitions)) p.add(X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, [...at, \"definitions\"], \"definitions is an array of exact Definition references\");
    else
      rawDefinitions.forEach((d: unknown, j: number) => {
        const ref = readDefinitionRef(d, [...at, \"definitions\", j], p, X.INVALID_FRAGMENT);
        if (ref === null) return;
        const usedBy = Array.isArray(componentKeys) && componentKeys.some((k: unknown) => typeof k === \"string\" && (uses.get(k) ?? []).some((used) => isSameExactDefinition(used, ref)));
        if (!usedBy || findBundleDefinition(bundle, ref) === null) {
          p.add(X.UNMAPPED_SOURCE, [...at, \"definitions\", j], \"a step may only cite exact bundle Definitions used by its own components\");
          return;
        }
        definitions.push(ref);
      });

    const rawBindings = own(r, \"bindings\");
    const bindings: CompilationFragment[\"bindings\"][number][] = [];
    if (!Array.isArray(rawBindings)) p.add(X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, [...at, \"bindings\"], \"bindings is an array\");
    else
      rawBindings.forEach((b: unknown, j: number) => {
        const bat = [...at, \"bindings\", j];
        const br = readRecord(b, bat, p, X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, [\"target\", \"source\"], [\"target\", \"source\"]);
        const src = br === null ? null : own(br, \"source\");
        if (br === null || typeof own(br, \"target\") !== \"string\" || !isPlainObject(src) || typeof own(src, \"pointer\") !== \"string\") {
          if (br !== null) p.add(X.INVALID_FRAGMENT, bat, \"a binding is { target, source: { kind, …, pointer } }\");
          return;
        }
        if (...

const compat = (...allow: string[]) => [{ relation: \"TARGETING\", allow }];
const prerequisiteToken = (skillKey: string, minimumRank: string) => `${skillKey.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9]+/g, \"_\").toUpperCase()}_${minimumRank.toUpperCase()}`;
const prereq = (skillKey: string, minimumRank: string) => ({ prerequisites: [{ prerequisiteKey: prerequisiteToken(skillKey, minimumRank), kind: \"SKILL_RANK_AT_LEAST\", skillKey, minimumRank }] });
const duration = (allowed = [\"INSTANT\"], defaultValue: string | null = \"INSTANT\", sustainEligible = false, manaLockEligible = false) => ({ allowed, default: defaultValue, sustainEligible, manaLockEligible });
const successFailure = (success: readonly unknown[]) => [
  { outcomeKey: \"SUCCESS\", data: { operations: success } },
  { outcomeKey: \"FAILURE\", data: { operations: [{ kind: \"NO_EFFECT\", data: {} }] } },
];
const fixed = (contributionKey: string, currency: \"AP\" | \"MP\", amount: number) => ({ contributionKey, currency, data: { kind: \"FIXED\", amount } });
const perUnit = (contributionKey: string, parameter: string, amount: number, includedUnits = 0) => ({ contributionKey, currency: \"MP\", data: { kind: \"PER_UNIT\", parameter, amount, includedUnits } });

const conversion = (canonicalKey: string, rank: string, ap: number, destination: string, saves: string[], extra: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({ canonicalKey, definitionKind: \"MANEUVER_EFFECT\", schemaVersion: 1, payload: { effectFamily: \"TARGETING_CONVERSION\", resolution: { mode: \"SAVING_THROW\", data: {} }, outcomes: successFailure([{ kind: \"CONVERT_TARGETING\", data: { destination } }, { kind: \"FOUNDATION_DAMAGE\", data: { fraction: 1 } }]), keywords: [], parameters: {}, compatibility: compat(\"SINGLE_TARGET\"), costContributions: [fixed(`${canonicalKey}.ap`, \"AP\", ap)], rules: { construction: prereq(\"skill.martial\", rank), effect: { branch: \"MANEUVER\", effectSubfamily: \"TARGETING_CONVERSION\", targetCategories: [\"CREATURE\"], resolutionPolicy: \"OWN\", attackType: null, saveProfile: { validSaves: saves, selectionPolicy: \"MARTIAL_CONTROL\" }, parameters: [], formulas: [], duration: duration(), targetingConversion: { destination: { $ref: destination } }, restrictions: [], ...extra } } } });

  value is authored. M5-WO10 must resolve it before complete Projectile Spell pricing can pass.

The delivery check is distinct from Effect outcome; an ATTACK_ROLL Effect reuses the same roll, while a SAVING_THROW Effect resolves its Effect-owned Save only after delivery hits.

required, exact, executable and OWNED by this exact profile (§80–§85, §135–§139). */%0Afunction governingOf(composition: AbilityComposition, index: AffinityDefinitionIndex, profile: CharacterAffinityProfile, p: Problems): Governing | null {%0A  if (composition.affinity === null) {%0A    p.add(A.GOVERNING_AFFINITY_REQUIRED, [\"subject\", \"composition\", \"affinity\"], \"executable Affinity evaluation needs one explicit governing Affinity; none is ever auto-selected (Primary, highest rank, first match or newest)\");%0A    return null;%0A  }%0A  const ref = composition.affinity.definition;%0A  const entry = findAffinityEntry(index, ref);%0A  if (entry === null) {%0A    p.add(A.AFFINITY_NOT_EXECUTABLE, [\"subject\", \"composition\", \"affinity\"], \"the null) {%0A    p.add(A.GOVERNING_AFFINITY_NOT_OWNED, [\"subject\", \"composition\", \"affinity\"], \"the governing Affinity must be the character's exact owned Affinity Version; its rank always comes from the profile\");%0A    return null;%0A  }%0A  return { entry, owned };%0A}%0A%0A/** Strength and Weakness REQUIREMENT steps over the Ability's resolved Keyword set (§86, §90, §151–§152). */%0Afunction emitRules(e: AffinityEmitter, g: Governing, keywords: string) {%0A  const strengths: { rule: AffinityStrengthRule; fragmentKey: string; condition: JsonObject }[] = [];%0A  const weaknesses: { rule: AffinityWeaknessRule; fragmentKey: string; condition: JsonObject }[] = [];%0A  const binding = [fromFragment(\"/facts/keywords\", keywords, \"/data\")];%0A emptyFacts() }, bindings: binding, role: \"affinity-strength-match\", componentKeys: [GOVERNING], definitions: [g.entry.ref] });%0A    strengths.push({ rule, fragmentKey, condition });%0A  });%0A return { strengths, weaknesses };%0A}%0A%0Afunction readComposition(value: unknown, bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle, p: Problems): AbilityComposition | null {%0A  const out = null;%0A  }%0A  return out.value;%0A}%0A%0A/** Compiles one Affinity subject into an M4 EvaluationPlan. Pure, deterministic; never mutates or retains its inputs. */%0Aexport function compileAffinity(request: unknown): ProwessOutcome<ProwessAffinityCompiledPlan> {%0A  const p = new Problems();%0A  if null) return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A  const contractVersion = own(r, \"contractVersion\");%0A  if (typeof contractVersion !== \"number\" || !SUPPORTED_PROWESS_COMPILATION_CONTRACT_VERSIONS.includes(contractVersion)) {%0A    p.add(X.UNSUPPORTED_CONTRACT_VERSION, [\"contractVersion\"], `supported Prowess compilation contract versions: ${SUPPORTED_PROWESS_COMPILATION_CONTRACT_VERSIONS.join(\", \")}`);%0A  }%0A  const subject = readSubject(own(r, \"subject\"), p);%0A  // The bundle is re-validated against the exact RulesetContext (the WO2 / WO3 rule): never trusted as supplied.%0A  const ruleset = createRulesetContext(own(r, \"ruleset\"));%0A  const rawBundle = own(r, \"definitions\");%0A  let bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle | => ({ affinity: g.entry.ref, identity: g.entry.affinity, rank: g.owned.rank, role: g.owned.role, rankRule: index.rankRule.ref });%0A%0A  switch (subject.kind) {%0A    case \"AFFINITY_ALIGNMENT\":%0A }%0A    if (ref === null || rank === null || role === null || p.count > before) return;%0A    if (ref.definitionKind !== \"AFFINITY\" || findBundleDefinition(bundle, ref) === null) {%0A });%0A  const primaries = owned.filter((o) => o.role === \"PRIMARY\");%0A  if (p.count === 0 && primaries.length !== 1) p.add(A.INVALID_AFFINITY_PROFILE, [\"affinities\"], \"a profile has exactly one (a.role === \"PRIMARY\" ? -1 : 1) : a.affinity.canonicalKey < b.affinity.canonicalKey ? -1 : a.affinity.canonicalKey > b.affinity.canonicalKey ? 1 : 0));%0A  return finish(p, () => deepFreeze({ primaryAffinity: (primaries[0] as OwnedAffinity).affinity, affinities: ordered }));%0A}%0A%0A/** The character's exact owned entry for `ref`, or null (exact version equality — §139). */%0Aexport function ownedAffinity(profile: CharacterAffinityProfile, ref: ProwessDefinitionRef): OwnedAffinity | null {%0A  return profile.affinities.find((o) => o.affinity.entityId === ref.entityId && o.affinity.entityVersionId === ref.entityVersionId && o.affinity.canonicalKey === ref.canonicalKey) ?? null;%0A}%0A%0A// ---- standard progression validation (§157–§160) --------------------------------------------------------------------%0A%0A/** VALID: some authored progression path reaches the profile. REQUIRES_CANON: only a path through an unresolved option does. */%0Aexport type AffinityProgressionStatus = \"VALID\" | \"INVALID\" | \"REQUIRES_CANON\";%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityProgressionResult {%0A  readonly status: AffinityProgressionStatus;%0A  readonly characterLevel: number;%0A  /** The first authored path (Level → optionKey) that reaches the profile, when one does. */%0A  readonly path: readonly { readonly level: number; readonly optionKey: string }[] | null;%0A  /** Open source conflicts a REQUIRES_CANON path passes through (M5-SC-035). */%0A  readonly unresolvedConflicts: readonly string[];%0A  /** Profile entries accepted by an explicit, provenance-backed override instead of standard progression (§159). */%0A  readonly overridden: readonly { readonly overrideKey: string; readonly affinity: ProwessDefinitionRef; readonly rank: AffinityRank; readonly sourceKey: string }[];%0A  /** The options the next authored Level offers, exposed structurally (an unresolved option is never auto-executed). */%0A  readonly nextOptions: { readonly level: number; readonly options: readonly AffinityAdvancementOption[] } | null;%0A}%0A%0Ainterface Slot {%0A  readonly role: AffinityRole;%0A  readonly rank: AffinityRank | null;%0A}%0Ainterface PathState {%0A  readonly slots: readonly Slot[];%0A  readonly path: readonly { readonly level: number; readonly optionKey: string }[];%0A  readonly conflicts: readonly string[];%0A}%0A%0Aconst slotKey = (s: Slot) => `${s.role}:${s.rank ?? \"?\"}`;%0Aconst stateKey = (s: PathState) => s.slots.map(slotKey).sort().join(\"|\");%0A%0Afunction applyOption(state: PathState, option: AffinityAdvancementOption, level: number): PathState[] {%0A  const step = [...state.path, { level, optionKey: option.optionKey }];%0A  switch (option.action) {%0A    case \"CHOOSE_PRIMARY_AFFINITY\":%0A      return state.slots.some((s) => s.role === \"PRIMARY\") ? [] : [{ slots: [...state.slots, { role: \"PRIMARY\", rank: option.startingRank }], path: step, conflicts: state.conflicts }];%0A    case \"RANK_UP\": {%0A      const out: PathState[] = [];%0A      state.slots.forEach((s, i) => {%0A        if (s.rank !== option.from || (option.target === \"PRIMARY\" && s.role !== \"PRIMARY\")) return;%0A        out.push({ slots: state.slots.map((x, j) => (j === i ? { role: x.role, rank: option.to } : x)), path: step, conflicts: state.conflicts });%0A      });%0A      return out;%0A    }%0A    case \"GAIN_AFFINITY\":%0A      return [{ slots: [...state.slots, { role: option.role, rank: option.startingRank }], path: step, conflicts: option.conflictKey === null ? state.conflicts : [...state.conflicts, option.conflictKey] }];%0A  }%0A}%0A%0A/** Whether `wanted` (the profile's role / rank slots) is exactly `state`, an unresolved (null) rank matching any rank. */%0Afunction matches(state: PathState, wanted: readonly Slot[]): boolean {%0A  const remaining = [...wanted];%0A  const wildcards: Slot[] = [];%0A  for (const s of state.slots) {%0A    if (s.rank === null) {%0A      wildcards.push(s);%0A      continue;%0A    }%0A    const at = remaining.findIndex((w) => w.role === s.role && w.rank === s.rank);%0A    if (at === -1) return false;%0A    remaining.splice(at, 1);%0A  }%0A  for (const w of wildcards) {%0A    const at = remaining.findIndex((x) => x.role === w.role);%0A    if (at === -1) return false;%0A    remaining.splice(at, 1);%0A  }%0A  return remaining.length === 0;%0A}%0A%0A/**%0A * Validates a profile against the exact AFFINITY_PROGRESSION Definition at a Character Level (§157–§160). The authored%0A * Levels are applied in order, every option of a CHOOSE_ONE Level branching; the profile is VALID when some path%0A * reaches exactly its (role, rank) slots. Progression is never inferred from the Level alone (§52): this only checks%0A * that the explicit profile is REACHABLE. An unresolved starting rank (M5-SC-035) matches structurally but yields%0A * REQUIRES_CANON — it is never guessed (§49). Overrides remove named entries from the standard check, explicitly.%0A */%0Aexport function validateAffinityProgression(input: { readonly definitions: ProwessDefinitionBundle; readonly profile: unknown; readonly characterLevel: unknown; readonly overrides?: unknown }): ProwessOutcome<AffinityProgressionResult> {%0A  const p = new Problems();%0A  const index = indexAffinityDefinitions(input.definitions);%0A  if (!index.ok) return index as ProwessOutcome<never>;%0A  const progression = index.value.progression;%0A  if (progression === null) {%0A    p.add(A.MISSING_AFFINITY_DEFINITION, [\"definitions\"], \"the bundle has no AFFINITY_PROGRESSION Definition\");%0A    return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A  }%0A  const profile = validateAffinityProfile(input.profile, input.definitions, index.value);%0A  if (!profile.ok) {%0A    for (const problem of profile.problems) p.list.push({ ...problem, path: `/profile${problem.path}` });%0A    return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A  }%0A  const level = input.characterLevel;%0A  if (typeof level !== \"number\" || !Number.isSafeInteger(level) || !(level >= FIRST_CHARACTER_LEVEL)) {%0A    p.add(A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, [\"characterLevel\"], \"characterLevel is an integer of at least 1\");%0A    return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A  }%0A  const overridden: => null as never);%0A    if (!Array.isArray(input.overrides)) p.add(A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, [\"overrides\"], \"overrides is an array of { overrideKey, affinity, rank, sourceKey }\");%0A    else%0A      input.overrides.forEach((raw: unknown, i: number) => {%0A        const fields = [\"overrideKey\", \"affinity\", \"rank\", \"sourceKey\"];%0A        const o = readRecord(raw, [\"overrides\", i], p, A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, fields, fields);%0A        if (o === null) return;%0A        const overrideKey = own(o, \"overrideKey\");%0A        const sourceKey = own(o, \"sourceKey\");%0A        const ref = readDefinitionRef(own(o, \"affinity\"), [\"overrides\", i, \"affinity\"], p, A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST);%0A        const rank = readEnum(own(o, \"rank\"), AFFINITY_RANKS, [\"overrides\", i, \"rank\"], p, A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, \"Affinity Rank\");%0A        if (!isToken(overrideKey)) p.add(A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, [\"overrides\", i, \"overrideKey\"], \"overrideKey is a SCREAMING_SNAKE token\");%0A        // An override is provenance-backed: it names the exact canonical key of the feature that grants it (§159).%0A        if (!isExactCanonicalKey(sourceKey)) p.add(A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, [\"overrides\", i, \"sourceKey\"], \"sourceKey is the exact canonical key of the rule granting the override\");%0A        if (ref === null || rank === null || !isToken(overrideKey) || !isExactCanonicalKey(sourceKey)) return;%0A        const target = profile.value.affinities.find((a) => a.affinity.entityVersionId === ref.entityVersionId && a.affinity.entityId === ref.entityId && a.rank === rank);%0A        if (target === undefined) p.add(A.INVALID_PROGRESSION_REQUEST, [\"overrides\", i], \"an override names an exact owned Affinity at its owned rank\");%0A        else overridden.push({ overrideKey, affinity: target.affinity, rank, sourceKey });%0A      });%0A  }%0A  if (p.count > 0) return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A%0A  const wanted: Slot[] = profile.value.affinities%0A    .filter((a) => !overridden.some((o) => o.affinity.entityId === a.affinity.entityId))%0A    .map((a) => ({ role: a.role, rank: a.rank }));%0A  let states: PathState[] = [{ slots: [], path: [], conflicts: [] }];%0A  for (const step of progression.payload.levels) {%0A    if (step.level > level) break;%0A    const next = new Map<string, PathState>();%0A    for (const state of states) for (const option of step.options) for (const s of applyOption(state, option, step.level)) {%0A      const key = `${stateKey(s)}#${s.conflicts.join()}`;%0A      if (!next.has(key)) next.set(key, s);%0A    }%0A    states = [...next.values()];%0A  }%0A  const resolvedHit = states.find((s) => s.slots.every((x) => x.rank !== null) && matches(s, wanted));%0A  const unresolvedHit = resolvedHit === undefined ? states.find((s) => matches(s, wanted)) : undefined;%0A  const hit = resolvedHit ?? unresolvedHit;%0A  const upcoming = progression.payload.levels.find((l) => l.level > level);%0A  return finish(p, () =>%0A    deepFreeze({%0A      status: resolvedHit !== undefined ? (\"VALID\" as const) : unresolvedHit !== undefined ? (\"REQUIRES_CANON\" as const) : (\"INVALID\" as const),%0A      characterLevel: level,%0A      path: hit === undefined ? null : hit.path,%0A      unresolvedConflicts: unresolvedHit === undefined ? Every value is COPIED from an M4 step%0A * response: whether a rule matched (an M4 Requirement), which exact Keyword matched and who contributed it (the M4%0A * KEYWORD set, looked up through M4's own exact query), the selected highest Weakness (an M4 MAX), and each cost value%0A * before / after (M4 modifier outcomes). The reader never recomputes, rounds or re-derives a number (§99, §127).%0A * This is a pure structural reading — not a second RuleEngineResponse contract (§153).%0A */%0Aexport interface AffinityKeywordSource {%0A  readonly componentKey: string;%0A  readonly contract: AbilityComponentContract | null;%0A  readonly definition: ProwessDefinitionRef | null;%0A}%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityMatchedKeyword {%0A  readonly keywordId: string;%0A  readonly canonicalKey: string;%0A  /** Every explicit contributor of this Keyword, in M4 contributor order (§66, §150). */%0A  readonly contributors: readonly AffinityKeywordSource[];%0A}%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityRuleMatch {%0A  readonly ruleKey: string;%0A  readonly matchAny: readonly string[];%0A  readonly matched: boolean;%0A  readonly matchedKeywords: readonly AffinityMatchedKeyword[];%0A}%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityWeaknessMatch extends AffinityRuleMatch {%0A  readonly activationPenaltyPercent: number;%0A  readonly severity: AffinityWeaknessSeverity | null;%0A}%0A%0A/** The pure Affinity alignment result (§78). Every matched rule is kept even when only one penalty applies (§92, §152). */%0Aexport interface AffinityAlignmentResult {%0A  readonly governingAffinity: { readonly affinity: ProwessDefinitionRef; readonly identity: CanonicalAffinity; readonly rank: AffinityRank; readonly role: AffinityRole; readonly rankRule: ProwessDefinitionRef };%0A  readonly alignment: AffinityAlignment;%0A  readonly strengths: readonly AffinityRuleMatch[];%0A  readonly weaknesses: readonly AffinityWeaknessMatch[];%0A  /** The Strength discount M4 actually applied (SPELL_AFFINITY_COST only; 0 when unmatched or at rank NONE). */%0A  readonly effectiveStrengthDiscountPercent: number | null;%0A  /** The highest matched Weakness percentage M4 selected (SPELL_AFFINITY_COST only; 0 when none matched). */%0A  readonly selectedWeaknessPercent: number | null;%0A  readonly issues: readonly string[];%0A}%0A%0A/**%0A * One percentage modifier's M4 outcome on the NET cost percentage (designer ruling M5-SC-028): `before` / `after` are%0A * percentages of the listed cost (100 = full price), never MP.%0A */%0Aexport interface AffinityCostStage {%0A  readonly modifierKey: string;%0A  readonly operation: string;%0A  readonly percent: number;%0A  readonly applied: boolean;%0A  readonly before: number;%0A  readonly after: number;%0A}%0A%0A/** The Affinity-adjusted listed cost (§79, §133). Not final MP: Casting Conditions and final rounding come later (§98). */%0Aexport interface AffinityCostReading {%0A  readonly costTarget: AffinityCostTarget;%0A  readonly baseCost: number;%0A  readonly strength: AffinityCostStage | null;%0A  readonly weakness: AffinityCostStage | null;%0A  /** The combined Affinity percentage of the listed cost M4 computed (e.g. 90 = a net −10%25), applied once (M5-SC-028). */%0A  readonly netCostPercent: number;%0A  /** Listed cost × net ÷ 100, unrounded — final rounding (normal, once) belongs to the end of Spell creation. */%0A  readonly affinityAdjustedCost: number;%0A}%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityAuthorityReading {%0A  readonly authority: SpellConstructionAuthority;%0A  readonly minimumRank: AffinityRank;%0A  readonly rankSatisfied: boolean;%0A  /** Null at a rank that grants no Authority capacity (NONE). */%0A  readonly effectCapacity: { readonly capacity: number; readonly count: number; readonly satisfied: boolean } | null;%0A  readonly traitCapacity: { readonly capacity: number; readonly count: number; readonly satisfied: boolean } | null;%0A  readonly effectAccess: readonly { readonly componentKey: string; readonly satisfied: boolean; readonly granted: string | null }[];%0A  /** Every Requirement satisfied. A failure is a valid result, never an engine error; nothing is truncated (§193). */%0A  readonly valid: boolean;%0A}%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityAccessReading {%0A  readonly requirementKey: string;%0A  readonly affinity: ProwessDefinitionRef;%0A  readonly minimumRank: AffinityRank;%0A  readonly satisfied: boolean;%0A}%0A%0Aexport interface AffinityRunReading {%0A  readonly alignment: AffinityAlignmentResult | null;%0A  readonly cost: AffinityCostReading | null;%0A  readonly authority: AffinityAuthorityReading | null;%0A  readonly access: readonly AffinityAccessReading[];%0A}%0A%0A/** Reads every named output of `plan` from an M4 EvaluationRun of exactly that plan. Fails closed on a foreign / incomplete run. */%0Aexport function readAffinityRun(input: { readonly plan: ProwessAffinityCompiledPlan; readonly run: unknown }): ProwessOutcome<AffinityRunReading> {%0A  const p = new Problems();%0A  if (!checkPlainData(input.run, [\"run\"], p, C.NOT_PLAIN_DATA)) return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A  const steps = isPlainObject(input.run) ? own(s, \"stepKey\") : null)));%0A  if (planned.size !== ran.size || ![...planned].every((k) => ran.has(k))) p.add(A.INCOMPLETE_RUN, [\"run\", \"steps\"], \"the run is not an evaluation of exactly this plan\");%0A  const data = (stepKey: string): Record<string, unknown> | null => {%0A    const step = steps.find((s: unknown) => isPlainObject(s) && own(s, \"stepKey\") === stepKey) as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;%0A    const response = step === undefined ? undefined : own(step, \"response\");%0A    const value = isPlainObject(response) && own(response, \"success\") === true ? own(response, \"data\") : undefined;%0A    if (step === undefined || own(step, \"executionStatus\") !== \"EXECUTED\" || !isPlainObject(value)) {%0A      p.add(A.INCOMPLETE_RUN, [\"run\", \"steps\"], `step ${stepKey} did not execute successfully`);%0A      return null;%0A    }%0A    return value;%0A  };%0A  if (p.count > 0) return finish(p, () => null as never);%0A  const o = input.plan.outputs;%0A%0A  const keywordSet = (stepKey: string | null): ResolvedKeywordSet | null => (stepKey === null ? null, definition: c?.definition ?? null };%0A  };%0A  const set = keywordSet(o.keywords);%0A  /** A rule's M4 outcome: satisfied, plus each KEYWORD_PRESENT leaf M4 found present, with its exact contributors. */%0A  const ruleMatch = (rule: AffinityCompiledRule): AffinityRuleMatch | null => {%0A    const d = data(rule.stepKey);%0A    if (d === null) return null;%0A    const outcome = own(d, \"outcome\") as Record<string, unknown>;%0A    const leaves = (own(outcome, \"children\") as Record<string, unknown>[]) ?? [];%0A    const matchedKeywords: AffinityMatchedKeyword[] = [];%0A    for (const leaf of leaves) {%0A      if (own(leaf, \"satisfied\") !== true) continue;%0A      const keywordId = own(own(leaf, \"expected\") as Record<string, unknown>, \"keywordId\") as string;%0A      const resolved = set === null ? null : findResolvedKeywordById(set, keywordId);%0A      if (resolved === null) continue;%0A      matchedKeywords.push({ keywordId, canonicalKey: resolved.keyword.canonicalKey, contributors: resolved.contributors.map((c) => sourceOf(c.source.sourceKey)) });%0A    }%0A    return { ruleKey: rule.ruleKey, matchAny: rule.matchAny, matched: own(d, \"satisfied\") === true, matchedKeywords };%0A  };%0A%0A  let alignment: AffinityAlignmentResult | null = null;%0A  let cost: AffinityCostReading | null = null;%0A  if (o.governing !== null && o.keywords !== null && input.plan.subject.kind !== \"AFFINITY_AUTHORITY\") {%0A    const strengths = o.strengths.flatMap((s) => {%0A      const m = ruleMatch(s);%0A      return m === null ? null : data(o.weaknessSelection);%0A      effectiveStrengthDiscountPercent = strength !== null && strength.applied ? strength.percent : 0;%0A      selectedWeaknessPercent = selection === null ? 0 : (own((own(selection, \"finalTargets\") as Record<string, unknown>[])[0] as Record<string, unknown>, \"value\") as number);%0A      const target = finalTargets.find((t) => own(t, \"targetKey\") === c.targetKey);%0A      const netTarget = netTargets[0];%0A      if (target !== undefined && netTarget !== undefined) cost = { costTarget: c.costTarget, baseCost: c.baseCost, strength, weakness, netCostPercent: own(netTarget, \"value\") as number, affinityAdjustedCost: own(target, \"value\") as number };%0A    }%0A    alignment = {%0A      governingAffinity: o.governing,%0A      alignment: anyStrength && anyWeakness ? \"STRENGTH_AND_WEAKNESS\" : anyStrength ? \"STRENGTH\" : anyWeakness ? \"WEAKNESS\" : \"NEUTRAL\",%0A      strengths,%0A      weaknesses,%0A      effectiveStrengthDiscountPercent,%0A      selectedWeaknessPercent,%0A      issues: [],%0A    };%0A  }%0A%0A  let authority: AffinityAuthorityReading | null = null;%0A  if (o.authority !== null) {%0A    const a = o.authority;%0A    const satisfied = (stepKey: string) => own(data(stepKey) ?? {}, \"satisfied\") === true;%0A    const effectCapacity = a.effectCapacity === null ? null : { capacity: a.effectCapacity.capacity, count: a.effectCapacity.count, satisfied: satisfied(a.effectCapacity.stepKey) };%0A    const traitCapacity = a.traitCapacity === null ? null : { capacity: a.traitCapacity.capacity, count: a.traitCapacity.count, satisfied: satisfied(a.traitCapacity.stepKey) };%0A    const effectAccess = a.effectAccess.map((x) => ({ componentKey: x.componentKey, satisfied: satisfied(x.stepKey), granted: x.granted }));%0A    const rankSatisfied = satisfied(a.rankStep);%0A    authority = {%0A      authority: a.authority,%0A      minimumRank: a.minimumRank,%0A      rankSatisfied,%0A      effectCapacity,%0A      traitCapacity,%0A      effectAccess,%0A      valid: rankSatisfied && effectCapacity !== null && effectCapacity.satisfied && traitCapacity !== null && traitCapacity.satisfied && effectAccess.every((x) => x.satisfied),%0A    };%0A  }%0A  const access = o.access.map((x) => ({ requirementKey: x.requirementKey, affinity: x.affinity, minimumRank: x.minimumRank, satisfied: own(data(x.stepKey) ?? {}, \"satisfied\") === true }));%0A  return finish(p, () => deepFreeze({ alignment, cost, authority, access }));%0A}%0A%0Aimport { createRulesetContext, findResolvedRule, type RulesetContext } from \"@prowess/rules\";%0Aimport { Problems, checkPlainData, finish, own, readRecord, type ProwessOutcome } from \"../internal/problems.js\";%0Aimport { BUNDLE_ERROR_CODES as B, DEFINITION_ERROR_CODES as C } from \"../definition/codes.js\";%0Aimport { definitionRefOf, isSameExactDefinition, readEnvelope, type ProwessDefinitionEnvelope, type ProwessDefinitionRef } from \"../definition/envelope.js\";%0Aimport { PROWESS_DEFINITION_KINDS } from \"../vocabulary/families.js\";%0A%0A/** The exact Ruleset identity a bundle was materialized from (PAS-10 M5-WO1 §84), copied verbatim from the context. */%0Aexport interface ProwessRulesetProvenance {%0A  readonly rulesetId: string;%0A  readonly manifestId: string;%0A  readonly manifestHash: string | null;%0A  readonly rulesetVersionLabel: string | null;%0A  readonly release: { readonly releaseId: string; readonly releaseNumber: number; readonly versionLabel: string } | null;%0A}%0A%0A/**%0A * Every exact Prowess Definition of ONE exact RulesetContext (§84–87, M5-I007–I009). Definitions are ordered by%0A * Entity id (the RulesetContext order) so equal inputs give deep-equal bundles whatever order they were supplied in.%0A * A bundle never holds a version its RulesetContext does not pin, and never mixes two Rulesets.%0A */%0Aexport interface ProwessDefinitionBundle {%0A  readonly rulesetProvenance: ProwessRulesetProvenance;%0A  readonly definitions: readonly ProwessDefinitionEnvelope[];%0A}%0A%0Aexport function rulesetProvenanceOf(ruleset: RulesetContext): ProwessRulesetProvenance {%0A  return {%0A    rulesetId: ruleset.rulesetId,%0A    manifestId: ruleset.manifestId,%0A    manifestHash: ruleset.manifestHash,%0A    rulesetVersionLabel: ruleset.rulesetVersionLabel,%0A    release: ruleset.release === null ? null : { ...ruleset.release },%0A  };%0A}%0A%0A/**%0A * Builds a bundle from an exact RulesetContext and Definition envelopes. Definitions cannot be injected\");%0A      return;%0A    }%0A    if (pinned.canonicalKey !== envelope.canonicalKey) {%0A      p.add(B.CANONICAL_CONFLICT, [...at, \"canonicalKey\"], `the pinned Entity's canonical key is ${pinned.canonicalKey}`);%0A      return;%0A    }%0A    if (!PROWESS_DEFINITION_KINDS[envelope.definitionKind].entityTypes.includes(pinned.entityType)) {%0A      p.add(C.ENTITY_TYPE_MISMATCH, [...at, \"definitionKind\"], `${envelope.definitionKind} cannot be stored as EntityType ${pinned.entityType}`);%0A      return;%0A    }%0A    const pv = envelope.provenance;%0A    const releaseId = ruleset.release === null ? null : ruleset.release.releaseId;%0A    if (pv.rulesetId !== ruleset.rulesetId || pv.manifestId !== ruleset.manifestId || pv.releaseId !== releaseId) {%0A      p.add(B.RULESET_MISMATCH, [...at, \"provenance\"], \"this Definition was materialized under a different exact Ruleset / Manifest / Release; Rulesets are never mixed\");%0A      return;%0A    }%0A    if (pv.sourceRulesetId !== pinned.sourceRulesetId || pv.sourceManifestId !== pinned.sourceManifestId) {%0A      p.add(B.RULESET_MISMATCH, [...at, \"provenance\"], \"pin provenance must equal the exact context's (sourceRulesetId / sourceManifestId)\");%0A      return;%0A    }%0A    const ref = definitionRefOf(envelope);%0A    if (accepted.some((a) => isSameExactDefinition(definitionRefOf(a), ref))) {%0A      p.add(B.DUPLICATE_DEFINITION, at, \"the same exact Definition version is supplied twice\");%0A      return;%0A    }%0A    accepted.push(envelope);%0A  });%0A%0A  return finish(p, () => ({%0A    rulesetProvenance: rulesetProvenanceOf(ruleset),%0A    definitions: accepted.sort((a, b) => (a.entityId < b.entityId ? -1 : a.entityId > b.entityId ? 1 : 0)),%0A  }));%0A}%0A%0A/**%0A * The bundle Definition `ref` names EXACTLY, or null. null;%0A}%0A%0A/** Whether a bundle was built from exactly this RulesetContext's identity. */%0Aexport function bundleMatchesRuleset(bundle: ProwessDefinitionBundle, ruleset: RulesetContext): boolean {%0A  const a = bundle.rulesetProvenance;%0A  const b = rulesetProvenanceOf(ruleset);%0A  const sameRelease =%0A    a.release === null || b.release === null%0A      ?
